However, it must be revised because (1) dyn.grant_programme.name retains the ward suffix while the definition and key_paths claim the programme should be keyed on the scheme name, creating contradictory programme identity; (2) the dyn.grant_programme.code guard ('only emitted when labels match after normalisation') is not implemented in the mapping steps; (3) dyn.grant_programme.locality_variant asserts the suffix is a delivery location without source evidence; (4) recipient_name_as_published is marked personal_data 'always' despite the definition saying it is not always personal and despite organisational recipients being emitted as personal. These are semantic errors or unsupported assumptions, not merely bounded risks. Currency inference and observed_at fallback are acceptable if explicitly bounded. Verdict: revise."
  }
}

2-critic.json

{
  "verdict": "revise",
  "findings": [
    "dyn.grant_programme.name definition (ontology.concepts['dyn.grant_programme.name'].definition) asserts that a ' - <suffix>' component (e.g. 'Lord Mayor's Community Fund - Coorparoo') is a ward/locality delivery variant of the same scheme. The source does not state this. The mapping itself contradicts the concept: mapping.fields[target=dyn.grant_programme.name].rationale says 'no suffix is stripped and no ward/locality meaning is asserted, because the source does not state that a \" - <suffix>\" component is a delivery location rather than a distinct sub-programme.' The concept definition must drop the locality-variant assertion or the mapping must implement it; as written the emitted dyn.grant_programme.name carries an unsupported semantic claim.",
    "dyn.grant_programme.locality_variant (ontology.concepts['dyn.grant_programme.locality_variant']) is declared as a concept but has no mapping field entry and no emitted observations (validation.fields has no row for it). It is a concept that is never populated and rests on the same unstated ward-suffix assumption. Either remove it or add a mapping with an explicit source-stated basis; currently it is dead ontology surface.",
    "dyn.grant_programme.code definition says the code is 'Only emitted when the grant_list label matches the grant field after Unicode NFKC, dash/apostrophe folding and case-folding; mismatches are flagged and the code is withheld.' The mapping (mapping.fields[target=dyn.grant_programme.code].steps) only does trim + regex_extract('^([^,]+),') + trim; there is no label-match guard, no NFKC/dash/apostrophe folding, and no mismatch flagging. Validation reports 111/111 emitted with 0 errors, so the guard is not implemented. Held-out ordinal 411 shows the risk: grant = 'Lord Mayor’s Community Fund – Northgate' (curly apostrophe, en dash) while grant_list = 'L, Lord Mayor’s Community Fund – Northgate'; the code 'L' is emitted without the promised normalised comparison.",
    "record_id strategy (mapping.record_id.rationale) claims key inputs are normalised (Unicode NFKC, dash/apostrophe folding, case-fold) before hashing. The held-out rows show typographic variants: ordinal 237 uses ASCII \"'\" and \"-\" ('Lord Mayor's Community Fund - Coorparoo'), ordinal 411 uses curly '’' and en dash '–' ('Lord Mayor’s Community Fund – Northgate'). The emitted entity_ref values preserve the raw variants ('grant_programme:brisbane_grants_new:Lord Mayor's Community Fund - Coorparoo' vs 'grant_programme:brisbane_grants_new:Lord Mayor’s Community Fund – Northgate'), so the same scheme would produce distinct programme entity refs and distinct record ids unless the normalisation is actually applied. The mapping does not show the normalisation step in any field transform, so the claim is unverified and likely false for programme identity.",
    "dyn.grant_award.recipient_name_as_published is declared personal_data 'always' in the concept, but the definition says it 'may be a natural person or an organisation' and 'is not always personal'. The emitted observations (e.g. ordinal 237 'Stones Corner Community Kindergarten Incorporated', ordinal 319 'Aspley East Primary School Parents and Citizens Association') are organisations yet carry privacy: 'personal'. This over-labels organisation names as personal data and contradicts the concept's own wording. Set personal_data to a conditional/unknown classification or document the conservative always-personal choice explicitly as a policy, not as a fact.",
    "dyn.grant_award.recipient_abn is declared personal_data 'never', but an ABN can belong to an individual (sole trader) and the dataset explicitly mixes individuals and organisations. The concept says the ABN 'may belong to an individual or organisation whose entity identity is unresolved'. Marking it 'never' personal is inconsistent with that statement; it should be conditional/unknown until recipient kind is resolved.",
    "observed_at (mapping.observed_at) uses fallback 'resource_last_modified', but the emitted observations set observed_at = '2026-11-22T22:37:19+00:00', which equals the snapshot retrieval time (metadata.retrieved_at), not a source-stated resource last-modified. The mapping notes say observed_at falls back to snapshot retrieval time; the mapping.observed_at.fallback value 'resource_last_modified' does not match the emitted behaviour. Reconcile the fallback label with what is actually emitted, and do not present retrieval time as a source-stated observation time.",
    "dyn.grant_award.amount_approved requires qualifier 'currency' and the mapping sets currency = 'AUD' with source_path null and no steps, i.e. an inferred constant. The concept text acknowledges this is inferred, but the qualifier is emitted as if source-stated. The qualifier should carry an explicit inferred flag (or the concept should state the qualifier is inferred in the emitted observation), otherwise downstream consumers will treat AUD as publisher-stated.",
    "The 'index' field is correctly unmapped, but the record_id strategy hashes (grant, grant_round, organisation_recipient, project, amount_approved). Two genuinely distinct awards with identical programme, round, recipient, project and amount would collide into one record id. No collision check is reported in validation. Add a collision check or document the collision risk as a bounded limitation.",
    "RECEIVES_GRANT is declared in ontology.relationships and proposal.relationships but is intentionally unpopulated; validation.relationships only reports AWARDS_GRANT: 111. This is acceptable as a declared-but-unpopulated relationship only if the mapping notes make clear it is not asserted; the notes do say so. No action required beyond keeping it out of emitted data."
  ],
  "semantic_risks": [
    "Programme identity is unstable across typographic variants: 'Lord Mayor's Community Fund - Coorparoo' (ASCII) and 'Lord Mayor’s Community Fund – Northgate' (curly/en dash) will produce different grant_programme entity refs and different record ids unless NFKC/dash/apostrophe folding is actually applied. The mapping claims this normalisation but no field transform implements it.",
    "The concept definition for dyn.grant_programme.name asserts a ward/locality-suffix interpretation that the source does not state and that the mapping rationale explicitly disclaims. This is an unsupported semantic assumption embedded in the ontology.",
    "dyn.grant_programme.locality_variant is declared but never emitted; it is dead ontology surface resting on the same unstated suffix assumption.",
    "The code-emission guard described in dyn.grant_programme.code is not implemented; codes are emitted for every row regardless of label match, so a mismatched grant_list/grant pair would silently yield a wrong code.",
    "Personal-data classification is inconsistent: recipient_name_as_published is marked 'always' personal even for organisation names, and recipient_abn is marked 'never' personal even though ABNs can belong to individuals. Both are over/under-labelled relative to the concept text.",
    "observed_at is set to snapshot retrieval time while the mapping fallback label says 'resource_last_modified'; the emitted value is not a source-stated observation time and should not be presented as one.",
    "Currency AUD is an inferred constant qualifier with no source path; it is emitted without an explicit inferred marker on the qualifier.",
    "Record-id collision risk for identical (programme, round, recipient, project, amount) tuples is not checked or documented as a bounded limitation."
  ],
  "checked_reuse": [
    "entity.abn: correctly not reused for the award-level ABN because recipient identity is unresolved; dyn.grant_award.recipient_abn is the right award-owned concept. Reuse deferred until resolution, as stated.",
    "ext.entity.holder_kind: correctly not emitted; recipient kind is not source-stated and the mapping does not infer it from names. Good.",
    "ext.financial.total_income / taxable_income / tax_payable / revenue_band: correctly not reused; grant award amounts are not recipient income. The concept text and mapping rationale both state this.",
    "ext.entity.sector_tag / ext.entity.industry_code: correctly not reused for project text or programme group; those are award/programme-owned, not business-owned classifications.",
    "ext.establishment.program / lga / region: correctly not reused for grant programme or locality variant; those are site-owned, not programme-owned.",
    "entity.legal_name / entity.trading_name: correctly not reused for organisation_recipient, which mixes personal and organisation names and is not a registered name.",
    "observation.source_record_id: correctly constructed deterministically rather than using the export 'index' ordinal; the 'index' field is correctly unmapped.",
    "observation.licence: correctly set to CC-BY-4.0 from metadata.",
    "observation.observed_at: reuse is questionable because the emitted value is retrieval time, not a source-stated time; see findings."
  ],
  "rationale": "The proposal correctly separates grant_award and grant_programme from business entities, avoids treating award amounts as income, avoids inferring legal form or company status from recipient names or ABNs, and correctly refuses to populate RECEIVES_GRANT and holder_kind without recipient resolution. However, it must be revised before acceptance: (1) the dyn.grant_programme.name concept definition asserts a ward/locality-suffix interpretation that the source does not state and that the mapping rationale explicitly disclaims; (2) dyn.grant_programme.locality_variant is declared but never emitted and rests on the same unstated assumption; (3) the code-emission label-match guard described in dyn.grant_programme.code is not implemented in the mapping, and held-out ordinal 411 shows typographic variants that the guard was meant to handle; (4) the record_id normalisation claim (NFKC, dash/apostrophe folding, case-fold) is not implemented in any field transform, so programme identity and record ids are unstable across the ASCII vs curly/en-dash variants visible in held-out rows 237 and 411; (5) personal-data classification is inconsistent (recipient_name_as_published 'always' personal even for organisations; recipient_abn 'never' personal even though ABNs can belong to individuals); (6) observed_at is set to retrieval time while the mapping fallback label says 'resource_last_modified'; (7) the AUD currency qualifier is an inferred constant emitted without an explicit inferred marker; (8) record-id collision risk for identical award tuples is unchecked. These are bounded, actionable revisions, not fundamental design errors, so the verdict is revise rather than reject. This is an agent review, not human sign-off."

3-critic.json

{
  "verdict": "revise",
  "findings": [
    "dyn.grant_programme.code definition (ontology.concepts.dyn.grant_programme.code.definition) states the code is 'Emitted only when the grant_list label matches the grant field after Unicode NFKC, dash/apostrophe folding and case-folding; mismatches are flagged and the code is withheld.' The mapping (mapping.fields[target=dyn.grant_programme.code].rationale) admits the label-match guard is NOT implemented and the code is emitted for every row. The emitted data confirms this: emitted_examples for source_row_ordinal 237, 319, 411, 486 all carry dyn.grant_programme.code='L' with no guard evidence. The concept definition therefore overstates what the pipeline does; it must be rewritten to match the actual transform (unconditional regex_extract) or the guard must be implemented.",
    "mapping.record_id.rationale claims 'Typographic variants (ASCII vs curly apostrophe, hyphen vs en dash) in programme or recipient text will also yield distinct ids because no normalisation step is applied in the field transforms'.     "The same typographic instability is a real semantic risk, not merely a documented limitation: ordinal 237 has grant 'Lord Mayor's Community Fund - Coorparoo' (ASCII apostrophe/hyphen) while ordinal 411 has 'Lord Mayor\\u2019s Community Fund \\u2013 Northgate' (curly apostrophe/en dash).     "dyn.grant_award.recipient_abn is declared personal_data='never' in the concept, but the mapping rationale says the classification is 'unresolved' because an ABN can belong to an individual (sole trader).     "dyn.grant_award.amount_approved requires qualifier 'currency' and the mapping sets it to constant 'AUD' with source_path=null.     "observation.observed_at is set to the snapshot retrieval time (2026-11-22T22:37:19+00:00) via fallback 'resource_last_modified', but the emitted observations show observed_at equal to ingested_at. However, the emitted data contradicts several proposal claims: the AUD currency qualifier lacks the promised inferred marker, observed_at is set to retrieval time and presented as source-stated, personal-data classification is asserted as 'always' for recipient names and ABNs that the definitions themselves call unresolved, the ABN invalid-value reporting is not visible, grant_list is both partially mapped and listed as unmapped, and RECEIVES_GRANT is declared but not emitted. The programme code extraction is unguarded and the programme entity refs fragment on typographic variants, both documented but not bounded in emitted data. National Pollutant Inventory

Operating footprint, facility-level ANZSIC/activity, operator identifiers and reporting history. Facilities table alone does not measure emissions or prove environmental compliance.

Downloaded source · Publisher documentation · Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International

Publisher: Australian Government Department of Climate Change, Energy, the Environment and Water. Retrieved 2026-11-22T22:37:19+00:00. Snapshot a0be4c37d588b391ea81baf240f675b2c9f3d9ee0210f9c451c7371eac11d6c7

New ontology concepts

ConceptOwnerTypeMeaning
dyn.establishment.npi_facility_idestablishmentstringThe national NPI facility identifier assigned by the National Pollutant Inventory, unique across all jurisdictions. Identifies the facility across snapshots; it is not the operator's ABN/ACN.
dyn.establishment.jurisdiction_facility_idestablishmentstringThe state/territory-scoped NPI facility identifier. Only unique within its jurisdiction_code, so it must always be qualified by the jurisdiction and never used alone as a key.
dyn.establishment.jurisdiction_codeestablishmentstringThe jurisdiction code under which the NPI registers the facility, as published. Usually an Australian state/territory code (NSW, VIC, QLD, WA, SA, TAS, ACT, NT), but the source reportedly also contains at least one non-state code ('COM'), so this is a free string rather than a closed enum; the raw code is preserved so the required qualifier on jurisdiction_facility_id is never dropped. Not guaranteed equal to dyn.establishment.state.
dyn.establishment.primary_anzsic_class_codeestablishmentstringThe ANZSIC class code the NPI states as the facility's primary activity classification. Describes the facility, not the company-wide industry. The scheme qualifier records the ANZSIC edition assumed for this code; the source column does not state an edition, so the qualifier is an inference, not a publisher-stated fact.
dyn.establishment.primary_anzsic_class_nameestablishmentstringThe ANZSIC class name the NPI states for the facility's primary activity classification, in the publisher's wording. The scheme qualifier records the ANZSIC edition assumed for this label; the source column does not state an edition, so the qualifier is an inference, not a publisher-stated fact.
dyn.establishment.main_activitiesestablishmentstringThe source-stated free-text description of the facility's main activities (e.g. 'Pig Farming', 'WINEMAKING'). Kept in the publisher's wording; not an invented industry code.
dyn.establishment.first_report_yearestablishmentstringThe NPI reporting-year label (e.g. '2003/2004') of the facility's first NPI report. A reporting-year label, not an incorporation or licence date, and not an observation date: all observations in a snapshot share the retrieval timestamp, so this label must not be read as when the record was observed.
dyn.establishment.latest_report_yearestablishmentstringThe NPI reporting-year label (e.g. '2024/2025') of the facility's most recent NPI report. A reporting-year label, not a compliance or licence date, and not an observation date: all observations in a snapshot share the retrieval timestamp, so this label must not be read as when the record was observed or updated.
dyn.establishment.report_countestablishmentintegerThe number of NPI reports the facility has submitted, as stated by the source. A count of reports, not an emissions measure or compliance indicator.
dyn.establishment.latest_report_idestablishmentstringThe NPI identifier of the facility's most recent report, as published. Identifies the report, not the facility.
dyn.establishment.latest_report_urlestablishmentstringThe published URL of the facility's most recent NPI report, as emitted by the website transform (a basic URL sanity check; the transform may normalise the value, so this is not asserted to be byte-for-byte verbatim). A link only; it embeds state/year/jurisdiction-facility path segments and is not a stable facility identifier.
dyn.establishment.street_addressestablishmentstringThe street address line of the NPI facility site as published (e.g. 'Hermann Thumm Drive', 'CNR BUSSELL HWY & BOUNDARY ROAD'). A site address, not the operator's registered office. The mapping applies trim and collapse_whitespace only; whether collapse_whitespace normalises U+00A0 (non-breaking space) is not asserted, so a non-breaking space or other non-ASCII whitespace may be preserved in the emitted value.
dyn.establishment.suburbestablishmentstringThe suburb/locality of the NPI facility site as published. A site locality, not the operator's registered locality.
dyn.establishment.stateestablishmentenumThe Australian state or territory of the NPI facility site address as published. A site address state, distinct from the NPI registration jurisdiction code. For the observed rows state equals jurisdiction_code, but the two are not guaranteed equal (the 'COM' jurisdiction case shows they can diverge), so they are kept as separate concepts. The mapping uses a closed 8-code enum with no passthrough: a state value outside that set is dropped (no claim emitted), not coerced.
dyn.establishment.postcodeestablishmentstringThe Australian postcode of the NPI facility site address as published. Observed values are 4-digit strings (e.g. '3169', '6155', '3300', '4428'). The postcode transform is treated as a 4-digit validator; no re-padding of 3-digit values is claimed or evidenced in the observed rows.
dyn.establishment.published_websiteestablishmentstringThe website the NPI publishes for the facility, as emitted by the website transform (a basic URL sanity check; the emitted value is a URL string, not reduced to a bare host). The domain may belong to the operator, a parent/group, or the facility itself (e.g. a group domain published against a differently named operator), so this is a facility-level published link and is not asserted to be the operator's own website.
dyn.business.registered_business_namebusinessstringThe registered business name of the operator as published by the NPI. This is a registered business name, which for individuals/partnerships is not a registered company legal name; it does not assert legal-name status or legal form. Blank in source is treated as missing (no claim emitted), not as an empty name.

Reuse and explicit omissions

Existing conceptJustification
ext.establishment.namefacility_name is the site/premises name, matching the establishment-owned site name concept.
ext.establishment.geolatitude/longitude are facility coordinates, matching the establishment geo point concept (lat,lon).
entity.abnabn is the operator's Australian Business Number, matching the business-owned ABN concept; validate checksum.
entity.acnacn is the operator's Australian Company Number, matching the business-owned ACN concept; frequently blank and some values fail checksum.
Columns deliberately left unmapped
ColumnReason
registered_business_nameBlank in 3 of 115 observed rows (source_present=112/115). Treated as genuinely missing in source rather than a reader/quoting artefact: the reader is a plain UTF-8 comma-delimited parse with no quoting loss observed elsewhere, and blank is mapped to missing (no claim emitted) rather than an empty name. This is the likely cause of the single missing OPERATES_ESTABLISHMENT edge (114/115), since the operator role has no name claim on that row; the edge is bounded to rows where the operator role emits at least one claim.
abnBlank in 12 of 115 observed rows (source_present=103/115). Blank is mapped to missing (no claim emitted); presence of an ABN is not inferred and no legal form is derived from it.
acnBlank in 25 of 115 observed rows and checksum-invalid in 2 (ACN_CHECKSUM: 776225038, 000435635). Blank and invalid values are not emitted as valid ACNs. Because the operator entity key joins abn+acn+registered_business_name with no normalisation, the same operator can fragment into multiple entity_refs across rows (with/without ACN); this is a documented identity limitation, not a hidden one.
stateMapped through a closed 8-code enum with passthrough=false and default=null. Any state value outside NSW/VIC/QLD/WA/SA/TAS/ACT/NT is dropped (no claim emitted), not coerced. Validation shows 115/115 emitted for this snapshot, so the drop is bounded here but not for future snapshots.
jurisdiction_codeKept as a free string (trim+upper only, no enum mapping) so non-state codes are preserved. The source reportedly contains a 'COM' code, but no emitted example containing 'COM' is available in the observed snapshot, so the preservation claim is stated as 'source reportedly contains' rather than evidenced.
primary_anzsic_class_codeThe scheme qualifier 'anzsic2006' is an inference: the source column does not state an ANZSIC edition. The qualifier is recorded as inferred, not publisher-stated; if NPI uses a different edition the qualifier would be wrong.
primary_anzsic_class_nameThe scheme qualifier 'anzsic2006' is an inference: the source column does not state an ANZSIC edition. The qualifier is recorded as inferred, not publisher-stated.
latest_report_urlPassed through the website transform, which may normalise the value (e.g. host casing or trailing slash). The definition is bounded to 'as emitted by the website transform', not byte-for-byte verbatim; the observed emitted values retain the full path segments.
facility_websitePassed through the website transform; the emitted value is a URL string, not reduced to a bare host. The concept is typed string and is not reused as ext.contact.website_domain (which holds a registrable host).
street_addressMapped with trim+collapse_whitespace only. Whether collapse_whitespace normalises U+00A0 is not asserted; a non-breaking space or trailing tab may be preserved in the emitted value (held-out ordinal 373 shows 'HENTY\u00a0HWY\t'). No normalisation beyond trim+collapse_whitespace is claimed.
postcodeMapped through the postcode transform, treated as a 4-digit validator. No re-padding of 3-digit values is claimed or evidenced in the observed rows.
first_report_yearA reporting-year label, not an observation date. All observations in a snapshot share the retrieval timestamp (observed_at falls back to retrieved_at, 2026-11-22T22:37:19+00:00), so this label must not be read as when the record was observed.
latest_report_yearA reporting-year label, not an observation date. All observations in a snapshot share the retrieval timestamp (observed_at falls back to retrieved_at, 2026-11-22T22:37:19+00:00), so this label must not be read as when the record was observed or updated.

4-critic.json

{
  "verdict": "revise",
  "findings": [
    "validation.fields[2] (dyn.establishment.jurisdiction_code): 1 ENUM_UNMAPPED error with example 'COM'. The enum values list only the eight AU states/territories, but the source contains at least one other jurisdiction code. The mapping's map_enum uses passthrough=false and default=null, so this row silently loses its jurisdiction_code and, because dyn.establishment.jurisdiction_facility_id requires jurisdiction_code as a qualifier, the qualifier is also dropped for that row. Either add the observed code(s) to the enum (with a definition that covers non-state jurisdictions) or document the drop explicitly; do not leave an unexplained unmapped value.",
    "validation.fields[19] (entity.acn): 2 ACN_CHECKSUM errors (examples '776225038', '000435635'). The mapping claims checksum-validated ACNs, but the emitted set still contains invalid values or the errors are only counted, not suppressed. State whether invalid ACNs are dropped (and then why they appear in error_examples) or retained; the current rationale 'checksum-validated' overstates what the pipeline does.",
    "proposal.reuse['entity.website'] maps facility_website to entity.website (business-owned). The column is named facility_website and the held-out row 15 shows 'http://www.cleanaway.com.au' for operator 'A.J. BAXTER PROPRIETARY LIMITED' — the domain does not match the registered business name, so the site may be a parent/group or facility-level site, not the operator's own website. This is an ownership assumption not supported by the source. Either bound the claim (e.g. note it is the published facility website, possibly group-level) or map it to an establishment-owned concept.",
    "proposal.reuse['entity.legal_name'] maps registered_business_name to entity.legal_name with claim_scope 'current_legal_name'. The source field is a registered *business* name, which for individuals/partnerships (e.g. 'A C PENTECOST', 'A L & A K BOSSE' per identity_cautions) is not a registered company legal name. Using entity.legal_name asserts a legal-name status the source does not state. Consider a business-name concept or explicitly bound the claim scope to 'registered business name as published'.",
    "mapping.entities.operator.key_paths = ['abn','acn','registered_business_name'] joins raw values. Because ACN is frequently blank and 2 ACNs fail checksum, the same operator can produce different entity_refs across rows (e.g. one row with ACN, one without), fragmenting operator identity. The execution contract says keys join raw trimmed values with no normalization, so this fragmentation is real. Document it or choose a more stable key (e.g. ABN alone when present).",
    "mapping.observed_at uses fallback 'resource_last_modified' but the metadata block has no last-modified field; per the execution contract the fallback is retrieved_at. The emitted observed_at equals retrieved_at (2026-11-22T22:37:19+00:00), which is observation-at-retrieval, not a publisher-stated update. The mapping should state this rather than implying a resource modification time.",
    "proposal.unmapped lists source_path 'state' with reason 'Mapped to dyn.establishment.state', which is contradictory: a path cannot be both mapped and unmapped. Remove it from unmapped or correct the reason.",
    "dyn.establishment.state and dyn.establishment.jurisdiction_code are near-duplicates for these rows (held-out rows show state == jurisdiction_code). The proposal justifies keeping both, which is defensible, but the definitions should state the observed duplication and that they are not guaranteed equal (the 'COM' jurisdiction case shows they can diverge).",
    "dyn.establishment.postcode is typed string with a 'postcode' transform; held-out postcodes are 4-digit strings ('3169','6155','3300','4428'). The transform's NT re-padding behavior is asserted in the rationale but not evidenced in the held-out rows; bound the claim.",
    "dyn.establishment.latest_report_url is mapped through a 'website' transform. The concept is a full report URL, not a registrable host; using a website transform may strip or normalize path segments. Confirm the emitted value preserves the full path (held-out example does preserve it), otherwise the 'link only' semantics are at risk."
  ],
  "semantic_risks": [
    "Operator identity fragmentation from the composite abn|acn|registered_business_name key when ACN is blank or invalid.",
    "facility_website attributed to the operator business may actually be a group or facility website (held-out row 15 domain mismatch).",
    "registered_business_name treated as entity.legal_name may overstate legal-name status for individual/partnership business names.",
    "jurisdiction_code 'COM' silently dropped, losing the required qualifier on jurisdiction_facility_id for that row.",
    "Invalid ACNs (2) may still be emitted despite the 'checksum-validated' rationale.",
    "observed_at is retrieval time, not a publisher-stated modification time; the mapping's fallback label is misleading."
  ],
  "checked_reuse": [
    "ext.establishment.name for facility_name — correct: site name, not operator name.",
    "ext.establishment.geo for latitude/longitude — correct: facility coordinates, geo_point validates AU bounds.",
    "entity.abn for abn — correct owner (business) and checksum transform present.",
    "entity.acn for acn — correct owner, but checksum errors remain (see findings).",
    "entity.legal_name for registered_business_name — owner correct but semantic scope questionable for non-company business names.",
    "entity.website for facility_website — owner questionable; column name suggests facility-level site.",
    "ext.establishment.source_key rejected in favour of dyn.establishment.npi_facility_id — justified: national vs generic publisher code.",
    "address.* (business-owned) correctly not reused for facility site address; dyn.establishment.street_address/suburb/state/postcode proposed instead — justified.",
    "entity.industry_code / ext.entity.industry_code correctly not reused for facility-level ANZSIC; dyn.establishment.primary_anzsic_class_* proposed — justified.",
    "ext.entity.date_established / entity.date_registered correctly not reused for first/latest_report_year — justified."
  ],
  "rationale": "The proposal is largely well-bounded: it correctly separates facility (establishment) from operator (business), keeps ANZSIC and address fields establishment-owned, avoids inferring legal form, and does not treat report counts as emissions. However, validation shows a real ENUM_UNMAPPED ('COM') that silently drops a required qualifier, two ACN checksum failures that contradict the 'checksum-validated' rationale, a contradictory unmapped entry for 'state', a misleading observed_at fallback label, and two ownership/semantic assumptions (facility_website -> entity.website, registered_business_name -> entity.legal_name) that are not fully supported by the source. These are bounded but actionable, so revise rather than accept. This is an agent review, not human sign-off."
}

      "mapping.entities.operator.key_paths = ['abn','acn','registered_business_name'] joins raw values. However, several claims are not fully bounded: the street_address NBSP/tab case (held-out ordinal 373) may survive collapse_whitespace; the operator key fragmentation and the single missing OPERATES_ESTABLISHMENT edge are documented but not explained; the 3 missing registered_business_name values are unexplained; the scheme=anzsic2006 qualifier is an inference not stated by the source column; and the 'verbatim' wording for latest_report_url and the URL-vs-host ambiguity for published_website conflict with the website transform.     "Source-stated new information is emitted: all 22 columns are read; state and jurisdiction_code are kept as separate concepts with the divergence risk (the 'COM' case) explicitly bounded rather than asserted as evidenced.",
    "Held-out rows corroborate the mapping: ordinal 15 (A.J. BAXTER, cleanaway.com.au against a differently named operator) supports keeping facility_website establishment-owned and not entity.website; ordinal 373 ('HENTY\\u00a0HWY\\t') is emitted as 'HENTY HWY' and the executed_transform_probe confirms collapse_whitespace normalises U+00A0, so the street_address definition's hedge ('may be preserved') is conservative rather than a false claim.",
    "ACN handling is honest: 2 checksum-invalid values (776225038, 000435635) are recorded as ACN_CHECKSUM value errors and not emitted as valid ACNs; the operator-key fragmentation from blank/invalid ACN is documented as a bounded recall limitation, not a hidden merge."
  ],
  "semantic_risks": [
    "The scheme=anzsic2006 qualifier is an inference; if NPI uses a different ANZSIC edition the qualifier would be wrong. It is labelled inferred, so this is a bounded risk, not an emitted false publisher-stated fact.",
    "dyn.establishment.state uses a closed 8-code enum with passthrough=false; a future non-state state value would be silently dropped (no claim emitted). Bounded here (115/115 emitted) but a recall limitation for future snapshots.",
    "The 'COM' jurisdiction preservation is stated as 'source reportedly contains' and is not evidenced in the observed snapshot; the free-string design is defensible but the preservation claim is unverified.",
    "Operator entity key joins abn+acn+registered_business_name with no normalisation, so the same operator can fragment into multiple entity_refs across rows (with/without ACN). Documented, but it is a real identity-recall limitation.",
    "The street_address definition says a non-breaking space 'may be preserved', while the executed probe shows collapse_whitespace normalises U+00A0 to a space; the emitted value is correct, but the definition's hedge is slightly over-cautious rather than wrong."
  ],
  "checked_reuse": [
    "ext.establishment.name for facility_name: correct site/premises name, establishment-owned, not conflated with registered_business_name.",
    "ext.establishment.geo for latitude/longitude: correct facility coordinate point, not ext.entity.address_point (business-owned).",
    "entity.abn for abn: correct business-owned ABN with checksum validation and blank->missing.",
    "entity.acn for acn: correct business-owned ACN with checksum validation; invalid values not emitted.",
    "Rejected reuse of entity.legal_name for registered_business_name: correct, since a registered business name is not a registered company legal name.",
    "Rejected reuse of entity.website for facility_website: correct, since the domain may be a group/facility site and the column is facility-scoped.",
    "Rejected reuse of ext.contact.website_domain for latest_report_url/published_website: correct, since those are full URL strings, not registrable hosts.",
    "Rejected reuse of address.* for facility address fields: correct, since address.* is business-owned and the NPI values describe the facility site.",
    "Rejected reuse of entity.industry_code/ext.entity.industry_code for primary_anzsic_class_*: correct, since those are business-owned and company-wide, while the NPI value is facility-level."
  ],
  "rationale": "The proposal and mapping are internally consistent, ownership-correct, and honestly scoped. Facility fields are establishment-owned, operator identity is business-owned, and no legal-name, legal-form, currency, or scheme-stated claim is invented. The only inference (scheme=anzsic2006) is explicitly labelled inferred. Time scopes are bounded (reporting-year labels vs observation-at-retrieval). The OPERATES_ESTABLISHMENT edge count is explained by missing operator claims, not a hidden guard. Held-out rows corroborate the facility_website and street_address handling, and the executed transform probe confirms collapse_whitespace normalises U+00A0 (the definition's hedge is conservative, not a false claim). Remaining risks (ANZSIC edition inference, closed state enum drop, unverified 'COM' preservation, operator-key fragmentation) are explicitly bounded and are recall limitations or labelled inferences, not evidenced semantic errors. No blocking finding remains.",

      "Rejected reuse of entity.legal_name for registered_business_name: correct, since a registered business name is not a registered company legal name.",
      "Rejected reuse of entity.website for facility_website: correct, since the domain may be a group/facility site and the column is facility-scoped.",
      "Rejected reuse of ext.contact.website_domain for latest_report_url/published_website: correct, since those are full URL strings, not registrable hosts.",
      "Rejected reuse of address.* for facility address fields: correct, since address.* is business-owned and the NPI values describe the facility site.",
      "Rejected reuse of entity.industry_code/ext.entity.industry_code for primary_anzsic_class_*: correct, since those are business-owned and company-wide, while the NPI value is facility-level."
    ],
    "rationale": "The proposal and mapping are internally consistent, ownership-correct, and honestly scoped. Facility fields are establishment-owned, operator identity is business-owned, and no legal-name, legal-form, currency, or scheme-stated claim is invented. The only inference (scheme=anzsic2006) is explicitly labelled inferred. Time scopes are bounded (reporting-year labels vs observation-at-retrieval). The OPERATES_ESTABLISHMENT edge count is explained by missing operator claims, not a hidden guard. Held-out rows corroborate the facility_website and street_address handling, and the executed transform probe confirms collapse_whitespace normalises U+00A0 (the definition's hedge is conservative, not a false claim). Remaining risks (ANZSIC edition inference, closed state enum drop, unverified 'COM' preservation, operator-key fragmentation) are explicitly bounded and are recall limitations or labelled inferences, not evidenced semantic errors. No blocking finding remains.",
    "blocking_findings": []
  }
}

Integration and limits

1,950 accepted source-scoped entity views; 12 exact-ABN link proposals to the existing sample; 527 business-role records left unlinked. These are record/entity views, not a count of distinct verified companies. Names alone never trigger a merge. Proposed links have not been hand-audited. Existing 50 profiles are not overwritten.
